Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the graphs framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 497. The resulting operator achieves bound O(793/n^1).

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to graphs theory, we decompose the problem into 497 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 793. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.
